  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">&nbsp;&lt; <a href="https://ocelot-spinach-2abg.squarespace.com/custom">JIB Format Home</a>&nbsp;</p><h1 style="text-align:center;white-space:pre-wrap;">JustInBasil Base Set</h1><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Below is a list of cards included in the first JustInBasil custom set for the Pokémon Trading Card Game. Although many of the cards below are reprints or spiritual reprints of existing cards, this set is entirely for fun and is not in any way official. All rights to Pokémon and the Pokémon Trading Card Game are © Nintendo, Creatures, GAMEFREAK, and The Pokémon Company International. Art included on each of these card mockups is the work of the illustrator credited on the card. Unofficial artwork can be visited at its source by clicking the artist’s name.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Click on a card to zoom in.</p>

Malamar<br></strong><em>From JustInBasil Base Set</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[D] Rule of Evil: This attack does 30 damage to each Pokémon that has an Ability <em>(both yours and your opponent's). (Don't apply Weakness and Resistance for Benched Pokémon.)</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">[D] Shadow Chant: 30+ damage. This attack does 10 more damage for each Supporter card in your discard pile. Hand Extension<br></strong><em>From JustInBasil Base Set</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">You can play this card only if you have more Prize cards remaining than your opponent.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Choose 1: </p><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Move an Energy from your opponent's Active Pokémon to 1 of their Benched Pokémon.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Switch in 1 of your opponent’s Benched Pokémon to the Active Spot.</p></li></ul><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Artist: Satoshi Yamamoto (Official Art, Colorized)</p>
</div>

Safari Zone<br></strong><em>From JustInBasil Base Set</em></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Once during each player's turn, that player may flip a coin. Then, that player shuffles their deck.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Artist: <a href="https://www.deviantart.com/mark331/art/POKEMON-Somewhere-in-Safari-Zone-588728477">mark331</a></p>
